ParlaMint-PL_2017-07-18-sejm-46-1.u52	... those who co-created these projects in the previous two terms of office. I will now move on to the presentation of the project's assumptions. [[Erase democracy.]] [[Why discuss it? In a word, sir.]] You can't insult me. Seriously, don't bother. [[Nobody insults anyone but you.]] I have a great sense of responsibility for the word, and I'm certainly not going to replicate your insulting phrases. [[Applause]] I'll go with the justification. The aim of the project is in particular to improve the functioning of the Supreme Court and to democratise the process of appointing judges to it. The new regulations are designed to ensure that people with the highest professional and ethical qualifications can serve as judges. Therefore, one of the most important elements of the project is the reform of disciplinary justice for judges, prosecutors, bailiffs, notaries and representatives of other legal professions, with the creation of a new autonomous Disciplinary Chamber within the Supreme Court. And this is, ladies and gentlemen, this change in the system of the court, a change within the meaning of Article. Point (b) of Article 4 (1) of Regulation (EU) No 1306/2013 is replaced by the following: 5. We don't change the state. We're changing the court's system. [[Applause]] [[Exactly. Exactly.]] [[Bravo, louder.]] And this constitutional provision, which you deliberately forget, is the correct argument, because it allows for the transfer of the judges' duties, also allows for the transfer to a resting state. The aim is to ensure maximum impartiality and effectiveness in the recognition of disciplinary matters, which are of great importance for the authority of the courts and in general the legal environment, as well as its morale. Disciplinary justice must be free from pressure from legal representatives and other negative manifestations of corporate solidarity. [[Applause]] [[Voice from the room: Exactly!]] [[It's called pseudo-olidarity.]] Hence the need for a reform involving the introduction of a new model for the operation of the Supreme Court. The nature, scope and large number of systemic changes prejudice the need for this new law, i.e. regulation of the entire organisation and functioning of the Supreme Court, as well as proceedings before the Supreme Court in a new, coherent, conceptual and structural act. This is necessary in view of the need for legibility of law in Poland, especially with regard to a constitutional body of the same importance as the Supreme Court. Here are the detailed solutions included in the project. The draft law consists of 8 chapters. Chapter 1 contains provisions relating to the scope of the Supreme Court's cognition and its specific location in the justice system, as well as the internal structure of that judicial authority. According to Art. 1 of the proposed law, the primary function of the Supreme Court is to supervise the case-law of general courts and military courts. This supervision is to be carried out through the examination of remedies and the resolution of legal questions. Another task of the Supreme Court is to uphold the rule of law and social justice in the area of justice. This new regulation directly refers to the constitutional principles of general principles, i.e. Article 2 2nd Constitution - I will remind you that it states that the Republic of Poland is a democratic legal state implementing the principles of social justice... [[It won't be long now.]] [[It's just stopped being.]] ...and to Article. 7 Constitution, which states that public authorities operate on the basis and within the limits of the law. Accordingly, it should be considered that in a democratic country where one of the fundamental constitutional principles is the rule of law, which is intended to implement certain values, it is necessary that the interpretation by the Supreme Court not only complies with the requirements of formal law, but also takes into account the axiological context in order to take into account values as well. In social life, beyond legal standards, there is the same system of standards and values that are not fixed in laws, but equally established, derived from morality or Christian values. [[Applause]] Social justice plays the role of principle, pattern, ideal, is the transfer of the ethical and legal idea of justice into the sphere of socio-economic relations. The conflict between the values and the applicable law creates a sense of injustice. In its judgments the Supreme Court should take into account this dualism. Currently, there are many judgments in the national legal system that are formally legal, but which are far from the judgment of the righteous and often create legal fictions contrary to the principle of material truth. This is unacceptable. [[Applause]] Imposition on the Supreme Court of the task referred to in Article. Point 1 (2) of the proposed law is therefore intended to restore a sense of elementary justice to citizens. [[Applause]] [[Voice from the room: Bravo!]] [[You liar!]] The implementation of this principle is intended to lead to judgments that are not only lawful but also true and social sense of justice. [[Applause]] Other powers of the Supreme Court include the proposal for disciplinary proceedings within the scope of a specific law, the recognition of electoral protests, including those relating to the election to the European Parliament, the determination of the validity of a national and constitutional referendum. This catalogue of tasks entrusted to the jurisdiction of the Supreme Court is open, as the proposed regulation provides that it may be entrusted with other activities set out in the laws. The project eliminates the jurisdiction of the Supreme Court to participate in legislative work by acting as an opinion. The projectors are aware that the Supreme Court belongs to a category of state authorities whose relationship with the other authorities, i.e. the legislative authority and the executive authority, is based on the principle of balance and independence. Since the Sejm and the Senate are predestined to create a law which, through the legislative process, must be approved by the President, the principal area of activity of the Supreme Court should be limited to the exercise of justice on the basis of that law. The project highlights the important role of the Supreme Court as a body appointed to settle disciplinary matters. This aims to put in place effective mechanisms for enforcing disciplinary responsibility. Legal literature shows the view that the basis for establishing disciplinary responsibility is to ensure that public tasks are properly performed in accordance with legal conditions. The functions of disciplinary responsibility should not be limited solely to this aspect, seeking further arguments for the establishment by the legislator of the liability of representatives of the various legal professions for conduct contrary to the responsibilities assigned to them or going beyond the rules of deontology of the profession or the dignity of the office. It should be pointed out that, in view of their functions, they are dealing with a good of particular social value, so the direct subject of protection of the rules of disciplinary law relating to judges is undistorted and compatible with the axiological reasons underlying the functioning of the judicial system, and indirect are the rights and freedoms of the individual in respect of whom proceedings under the law before courts constitute an instrument implementing the legal protection available to them. The proposal introduces significant changes in the internal structure of the Supreme Court in comparison with the state in force. It should be stressed that in accordance with Article. The Commission shall adopt implementing acts laying down detailed rules for the implementation of this Regulation. 2 Constitutional rules on courts and their jurisdiction and procedures leave the legislator - the legislator - with only a reservation that the matter referred to in Article. The Commission shall adopt implementing acts laying down detailed rules for the implementation of this Regulation. 2 must, by its nature, be governed by the rules generally applicable. I would like to stress here that the introduction of the Disciplinary Chamber is not something new. It's not something new in our legislation. Let me remind you that in the Law on the System of Universal Courts of 1928... At that time, in the interwar twentieth anniversary, there was no separate law on the Supreme Court, only the organization and functioning of the Supreme Court were placed in this law. At that time the Supreme Court was also a disciplinary court, from 1928 to 1939, although it may be worth knowing that in 1938 the Chamber for Lawyers was introduced in the Supreme Court. [[Jury ghetto. Don't plead for it.]] However, the monopoly of the law as a source of regulation of judicial organisations and proceedings before courts should be understood broadly. The exhaustive nature of the legislation does not preclude certain specific issues which are not relevant for the construction of the judicial system and their jurisdiction, the legislator has delegated to it by means of sub-laws. Exclusive statutory regulation in the area defined in Article. The Commission shall adopt implementing acts laying down detailed rules for the implementation of this Regulation. 1, as in other cases in which a person is qualified to regulate a specific matter in the act, it is therefore not absolute. This is the operative part of the judgment of the Constitutional Court of 15 January 2009, sygn. K 45/07. The proposed amendments are based on the assumption that the quality of the case-law should be harmonised with the optimally organised structure of the Supreme Court, adapted to the number of judges ruling in that court and serving a level playing field for judges, thus enabling them to carry out their judicial activities efficiently and without undue delay. It should be stressed that during the discussion on the shape of the current law on the Supreme Court... [[What kind of discussion?]] ...there were opinions about the need to simplify the organisation of this judicial authority and, consequently, to rationalise the system of administration of the Supreme Court. A separate Chamber of Labour, Social Insurance and Public Affairs has been proposed to resign... [[Who offered?]] ... showing that matters belonging to the scope of its jurisdiction... [[Who pointed it out?]] ...can easily be divided between the Civil Chamber and the Supreme Court Penitentiary. The planned solution partially implements these demands. According to Art. 3 of the proposed law The Supreme Court is divided into three chambers: the Chamber of Public Law, the Chamber of Private Law and the Disciplinar Chamber. The separation of the Chamber of Public Law and the Chamber of Private Law is an expression of taking into account the criterion in determining the cognition of a particular organizational unit of the Supreme Court and is a reference to the doctrine-based dichotomical division into public law and private law, based on the subject-matter of the regulation, the nature of the entities and the method of action of the law, i.e. shaping legal relations and legal situations. Art. 3 § 2 shall include a delegation to the Minister of Justice to issue a regulation specifying the total number of judges of the Supreme Court, the number of judges of the Supreme Court in each Chamber, the internal organisation of the Supreme Court, the detailed division of cases between the Chambers, provided that the Military Division is established in the Chamber of Public Law. I would like to remind you that the current Military Chamber consists of 6 judges per 83 Supreme Court judges, according to the first President of the Supreme Court of 2016 annual information. 6 judges in the Military Chamber, who in 2016 recognized only 88 cases for more than 11 thousand, which entered the Supreme Court in that year. It is therefore very appropriate to place this Chamber as a branch within the organisational structure of the Chamber of Public Law. The Disciplinary Board will recognise disciplinary matters of Supreme Court judges, general court judges, military court judges, prosecutors of the public office units of the prosecution, prosecutors of the Institute of National Remembrance and... I would like to stress here, because there is a misunderstanding here, at least that is the result of the public communication and speeches of some of the opposition Members. As far as professional authorities, lawyers, legal advisers, notaries, bailiffs are concerned, only exceptional remedies in the form of cassation will be recognised in this Disciplinary Chamber, if such cassations are affected by decisions of the courts and disciplinary committees of these professional authorities. The Chamber will also recognise labour and social security matters concerning Supreme Court judges and matters relating to the transfer of the Supreme Court judge to rest. A distinction in the structure of the Supreme Court of Justice of the Chamber appointed for the recognition of disciplinary matters of legal professionals will ensure that these cases are identified by a specialised, qualified and experienced judicial body, which will serve both the efficiency of these proceedings and the justice of their decisions and the public interest in harmonising the standards of conduct required for the representatives of the various legal professions. The Disciplinary Chamber immediately after its appointment will consist of 12 judges, and the target size of the judges of this House, as I said earlier, will be defined by the Minister of Justice in his regulation. The Disciplinary Board will consist of two departments. In particular, it would be appropriate for the first department to deal with cases of Supreme Court judges and cases of judges and prosecutors concerning disciplinary offences - but here, attention, at the same time, the exhaustive marks of intentional offences prosecuted by public prosecution - while the second department would consider appeals against judgments of disciplinary courts of first instance and provisions and regulations closing the way to the judgment, dismissals from disciplinary judgments of individual legal professions and appeals against resolutions of the National Courts Council. The Disciplinary Board will have a kind of autonomy, including the provisions of the project confer a specific legal position on its President. Legal regulations guarantee adequate legal status for persons who have judicial functions, providing them with adequate jurisdiction. The draft provides that the institutional guarantee of the impartiality of the judges of the Disciplinary Chamber is to be the special rules for the remuneration of judges in that Chamber and of judges delegated to carry out their duties in that Chamber. These judges are to be granted an allowance of 40% the basic salary and the functional allowance combined. Those judges will be bound by the prohibition to remain in another professional relationship or employment relationship and to refrain from taking another occupation or way of earning. Another amendment is to give the Minister of Justice the authority to issue a regulation laying down the rules of procedure of the Supreme Court. These Rules will determine the total number of judges of the Supreme Court, the number of posts in each Chamber, the internal organisation of the Supreme Court, the detailed division between the Chambers and the rules of internal proceedings. It should be emphasized here, that until now, according to the Law on the Supreme Court of 2002 the Rules of Procedure of the Supreme Court adopted the body of the judiciary, and previously in Polish law the regulations of the Supreme Court were implementing standards to the Law of 1928 - the Regulation of the President of the Republic of Poland, to the Law of 1962 - the Resolution of the Council of State, to the Law of September 1984 - the Decree of the Minister of Justice. I would like to stress here that the Supreme Court's Rules of Procedure were not published, it was not widely available. Only after an application has been submitted to the Constitutional Court concerning, inter alia, the first election, the President of the Supreme Court, has published these rules on the websites of the Supreme Court. The scope of the cases referred to in the Rules of Procedure of the Supreme Court concerns only administrative matters. The issue of acceptance of declarations of property of the first President of the Supreme Court and all judges of the Supreme Court was assigned to the President of the Republic of Poland - also an analysis of these declarations of property. Chapter 2 contains regulations concerning the bodies of the Supreme Court, the first President of the Supreme Court, the Presidents of the Supreme Court, each of these three chambers to be held in that court, the General Assembly of Supreme Court Judges, the Assembly of the Supreme Court Chamber Judges and the College of the Supreme Court. The project provides for a number of differences in the existing functions and tasks of local authorities. They relate in particular to the powers of the judicial authorities, i.e. The General Assembly of the Supreme Court Judges, the Assembly of the Supreme Court Chamber Judges and the College of the Supreme Court. Modifications in this respect are largely the result of the proposed changes in the procedure for the election of the first President of the Supreme Court, the Presidents of the Supreme Court and the candidates for the position of Judge in the Supreme Court. The main novel is the rules governing the special position of the President of the Supreme Court in charge of the work of the Disciplinary Board. These rules, as has already been mentioned, are a consequence of the planned model of organisation and functioning of the Disciplinary Board, which is intended to ensure its autonomy necessary due to the nature of the cases it considers. Designed art. 13 § 2 of the Supreme Court Act sets out the Circle of responsibilities of the Supreme Court President of the Disciplinary Chamber, including the management of the work and representation of the Supreme Court in matters relating to the functioning of the Disciplinary Chamber and the performance of the activities laid down in the Act, Rules and other normative acts. These tasks include, in particular, labour law activities and, in relation to persons performing business activities in the Disciplinary Chamber, administrative activities relating to the functioning of this Chamber, which have no connection with the exercise of justice, as well as powers relating to the substantive activities of the Disciplinary Chamber. In the light of the proposed regulations, the powers of the President of the Supreme Court in charge of the work of the Disciplinary Chamber must be: the appointment and dismissal of the Heads of Departments of the Disciplinary Chamber, the appointment and dismissal of the Head of Chancellery of the President of the Supreme Court in charge of the Disciplinary Chamber, the presentation of information on the activities of the Disciplinary Chamber, which the first President of the Supreme Court submits together with information on the activities of the Supreme Court to the President of the Republic of Poland and the National Council of Courts and the Sejm and the Senate, the presentation to the competent authorities of observations on irregularities found or gaps in the law, the removal of which may be intended to ensure the smooth recognition of matters belonging to the jurisdiction of the Disciplinary Chamber or the limitation of the number of disciplinary offences, the consent to transfer of a Judge of the Supreme Court to a position in another Chamber of Disciplinary Chamber and the transfer of a Judge to a position in the Disciplinary Chamber of Justice's office, or in the absence of a court's office, by a court of a court of a court of a court of a court of a court of a judge, of a court or of a court of a court of a court of law, of a court of a court of a court of a judge. 5 of the Act of 18 December 1998 on the Institute of National Remembrance. The next chapter, chapter 3, is a chapter that deals with establishing, changing and stopping the service relationship of a Supreme Court judge. The proposal simplifies and makes transparent procedures for the appointment of a Supreme Court judge and strengthens the position of the National Judicial Council in the process of appointing judges. The appointment of a Supreme Court Judge shall begin with a declaration by the Minister of Justice of the number of free judges to be held in each Chamber of the Supreme Court in which the vacant post of Supreme Court Judge shall be filled. This solution ensures not only transparency in the process of filling the position of the Supreme Court judge, but also takes into account the interests of those applying for or considering such an application, and thus serves to strengthen the guarantee that a Supreme Court judge will be entrusted to a person with a particularly high level of knowledge in this area of law, with which the scope of the jurisdiction of the Supreme Court Chamber in which that position is filled. The primary objective of the proposed amendments relating to the rules governing disciplinary action is to eliminate situations where procedural obstructions may arise from the faulty party. The practice so far indicates that the technique and strategy of the defender very often has only destructive features which constitute abuse of procedural rights and are directed towards slowing down the process and avoiding liability, delaying the time of the judgment until the expiry of limitation periods. We can cite examples of many such cases in which these facts have taken place. All this disciplinary action is also an amendment to the order judgment. If there is no doubt about it, you can issue a court order without questioning the defendant. Of course, he is entitled to an appeal after receiving such a court order, and then the cases will take place under the ordinary procedure. There is also an accelerated procedure for these proceedings, in particular for the purpose of examining the application for authorisation of a judge to be held liable in the event that the judge is caught in the act of committing the offence. At times, the immunity of a judge in this regard has been abused, and committees or disciplinary courts have found these cases at an irregular rate, dragging it away, often without setting such proceedings. It is also important to give the Minister of Justice the power to appoint one of the prosecutors, every time indicated by the national prosecutor, the Disciplinary Ombudsman of the Minister of Justice to deal with specific cases concerning a Supreme Court judge. The introduction of the Disciplinary Ombudsman institution of the Ministry of Justice is intended to ensure that an independent body not related to the environment concerned is involved and thus has the objectivity necessary for the proper exercise of the disciplinary action provided for by law. Participation in such an entity is intended to eliminate a situation where a disciplinary body from a given professional group could be guided by false professional honor or bias caused by professional solidarity in the performance of its tasks. It is up to the Disciplinary Ombudsman of the Minister of Justice to carry out investigations, initiate disciplinary proceedings, and to proceed to proceedings already in progress. The consequence of the regulation of the Disciplinary Ombudsman of the Minister of Justice is to assume that the participation of that entity excludes the participation of another Ombudsman in the proceedings. The aim of this solution is to prevent a conflict of competence which could arise if the disciplinary spokesman of the Minister of Justice and the disciplinary spokesman of the Supreme Court were granted the opportunity to act. The Disciplinary Ombudsman of the Minister of Justice shall have a stand-alone position in relation to the activities carried out, except where the Minister of Justice objects to the order to refuse to initiate disciplinary proceedings, which shall be equivalent to the obligation to initiate disciplinary proceedings. At that time, the indications of the Minister of Justice as to the continuation of the proceedings are binding on the Disciplinary Ombudsman of the Minister of Justice. The draft confers on the Minister of Justice similar powers over the decisions of the Supreme Court's disciplinary officer. The proposal also provides for a number of changes in the material and procedural aspects of disciplinary liability of Supreme Court judges. Here I would like to point out only the extension of the catalogue of disciplinary penalties by sanctioning the basic salary of a judge by between 5% and 20% for a period of between 6 months and 2 years. The same disciplinary penalty shall be imposed in the case of general court judges, prosecutors from state offices of the prosecutor's office and prosecutors who are IPN prosecutors. These are tasks governed by disciplinary rules. Another chapter to be discussed, I think, is the chapter on proceedings before the Supreme Court, chapter 6. It contains rules on procedural aspects of the exercise of justice by the Supreme Court. As a rule with regard to the number of formations of judges, three judges of the Supreme Court were accepted. In matters falling within the scope of the jurisdiction of the Disciplinary Chamber, which are recognised at the meeting, a single membership is provided for, while ensuring that the President of that Chamber can issue an order on the recognition of the case in the composition of three Judges. The composition competent to investigate labour and social security matters concerning Supreme Court judges and matters relating to the transfer of the Supreme Court judge to rest has been separate. The fundamental amendment contained in the proposed law concerns the procedure for adopting resolutions on legal issues. This amendment aims to shape the procedure before the Supreme Court in order to ensure as much uniformity as possible within the meaning of the law, and thus the uniformity of case law, and to eliminate the differences in interpretation within the Supreme Court itself, where, for example, two sets of three make contradictory judgments. The diversity of interpretation in resolutions against the same rules creates uncertainty, and there is no doubt that legal security is an essential feature of the democratic rule of law. The project provides for a number of solutions to achieve the above objective of ensuring the unity of the interpretation of the law. Firstly, it introduces increased requirements as regards the size of the formation of the court to adopt a resolution on the legal issue. According to art. 64 of the proposed law, if the Supreme Court, when considering the deletion or other appeal, has doubts, may defer the examination of the case and present a legal issue to the composition of the Chamber. In turn, resolutions containing the so-called. an abstract interpretation which refers to the legal standard and is not formulated in view of the need to resolve a particular case may be taken in the composition of the Chamber or the full composition of the Supreme Court, with 2/3 of the Judges present in each Chamber. Secondly, the proposal sets out the quorum necessary to take up this full composition and the full composition of the Supreme Court. In order to ensure the effective implementation of the obligation of the Supreme Court to ensure the uniform case-law of the Supreme Court, which is not only its statutory duty but also its constitutional obligation, which is immanent in its responsibility to supervise the courts of the lower instance, the project introduces a standard whereby the resolutions of the Supreme Court of the rank of legal principles are published in the Official Journal of the Republic of Poland. Ensuring a significant role of the Supreme Court in the exercise of disciplinary responsibility of legal professionals as one of the main objectives of this project is reflected in Article. 71 § 3 of the proposed law. In the light of the established rule of law, the Supreme Court may, in the event of a breach of jurisdiction, request that disciplinary action be brought before a disciplinary court, which shall in that case be the Supreme Court. In Chapter 7, the designer laid down the rules for the operation of the First President of the Supreme Court, the President of the Supreme Court, responsible for the work of the Disciplinary Chamber, and the Office for Studies and Analysis of the Supreme Court. The project is limited to identifying the basic principles for the organisation and functioning of the above-mentioned organisational cells, referring to the rules laid down by the first President of the Supreme Court and also by the President of the Supreme Court in charge of the Disciplinar Chamber, after consulting the College of the Supreme Court. The next chapter is chapter 8, the last chapter, which contains transitional and adaptation provisions. The draft provides for a number of fundamental changes with regard to the principles and disciplinary responsibility of general court judges and military court judges. At that time, the court of the second instance may, from the time the party has made such an application, suspend the enforcement of the judgment until the end of the cassation proceedings or make the enforcement of that decision conditional, and, if the appeal is dismissed, also the decision of the court of first instance, on the lodging by the claimant of the relevant security. In the absence of a cassation action, the order shall remain in force until the expiry of the time limit for lodging it. The order may be issued at a secret meeting.
